# Fixed goal accounting and progress

This is an implementation accounting view. The original user attachment is authoritative; changing this table cannot change its scope or completion criteria.

Attachment SHA-256: `11e35ba1f0faea89c183af307a4b4bd2dd9a6b1a8fbd63af8bac42d06e7a17f7`. Source: `/home/chia/.codex/attachments/e94d8875-9d86-482e-9bb3-8887aa457b88/pasted-text-1.txt` (636 logical lines).

The detailed numerical, scientific, ownership, communication and resource requirements remain the literal attachment text. [SCOPE.json](SCOPE.json) retains every literal line plus each row’s exact requirement and qualifications. [WORK_STATUS.json](WORK_STATUS.json) is separate mutable progress. [SOURCE_ALIASES.json](SOURCE_ALIASES.json) retains all source mappings and exclusions. [COVERAGE.json](COVERAGE.json) reports exact counts and unresolved applicability.

`NA` means **not_assessed**, never completed. A checked “source only” cell establishes only a component file’s existence. A checked “report only” cell preserves reported module-test evidence; it establishes neither the full named mode nor the production path. Excluded/not-required rows remain visible. Transport/helpers need typed native outputs and validation, not fabricated function-confidence scores.

No scientific analysis, network operation, job submission or test suite was run by this accounting task. Subsequent attributed verification reports are recorded separately below. The separate prior job-verification task is not imported into goal requirements.

## Unresolved applicability and source gaps

- **Q-BLAST-EXTRA-PAIRWISE**: Which additional native BLAST programs expose a meaningful two-sequence input contract? All six additional programs have retained database/native-program rows. Pairwise applicability is not inferred. Preserve their IDs and add mode children if native inspection establishes the contract.
- **Q-NATIVE-SERVICE-SUBMODES**: The attachment does not enumerate every native parameter/recipe/viral variant of named service families or B functions. Named families/functions stay fixed. Selected scientifically distinct modes require explicit child rows, their own contracts and parent identity. Upstream recipes are not silently promoted to additional required independent algorithms.
- **Q-ROUTE-FIT**: Current dependency, access, resource fit, native format and production binding are unassessed for most intended routes. Route candidates remain explicit and unfinished. Local client placement is separate from remote computation; no universal deployment claim.
- **Q-BV-FEATURE-SEQUENCE-SOURCE**: feature_sequence is absent from the historical BV-BRC collection prompt inventory. The required collection row is retained with source mapping not_assessed; do not substitute genome_sequence or drop the feature/sequence join.
- **Q-ENTREZ-APPLICABILITY**: Named provider/database scope does not prove every esearch/efetch/esummary method applies to every provider, especially Datasets. Four explicit wrapper rows remain bounded to named molecular databases; exact native applicability must be established before claiming a database/method binding.

Required rows without matching direct source inventory evidence: `D-L243-feature_sequence`, `E-L291-pfam`. Missing source evidence never removes a requirement.
